## Consequential readings

- 151_scan062, diagrams (15)/(17): preserve the solid, hooked and dashed arrows separately; the single/double shaft in (17) remains explicitly uncertain (p02, p04).

- 151_scan063, limit notation: the ink gives an underlined Lim without an arrowhead (p02).

- 151_scan063, lower endpoint comparison: the first upper endpoint is unprimed X_i, and the second endpoint pair is live below the cancellation (p25).

- 151_scan064, upper nonempty condition: the slanted insertion has no unambiguous leader to its V; both attachments remain marked (p08).

- 151_scan064, morphism list: the labels are alpha), (kappa alpha), (mu alpha); the right-hand endpoints, cancellation extent and live elementary-topos description follow the ink (p14, p18, p19, p21).

- 151_scan065, cardinality and arrow counts: the repeated small symbol is nu, with the inserted nu = card definition and its attachment preserved (p13, p16).

- 151_scan066: the upper-right NB diagram and the two-headed middle-left connection in (22) are retained; H omits the former and gives a single upward head for the latter. The star cancellation in (20) stays unresolved.

- 151_scan067: the d label in (23) carries a separate underline. The added d(I) phrase, fini et and inclusion in N are live; the cancelled superscripts and the state of I fini remain explicitly uncertain.

- 151_scan068: the middle X in (27) ends in -1, while H supplies -2. Its initial m/n remains doubtful. The starred V immediately above it reads n-1,n-2, and the source formula d(j)=j-1 is retained.

- 151_scan069, cancelled final equation: the right member reads U_{ij}; the left capital remains U/V. The three visible overbars in the preceding V expressions are retained against H’s unbarred readings.

- 151_scan070, axioms b and d: the manuscript has X_i<=X_j and a strict < below the union; the underlined h/k remains doubtful. Formula (2) retains a visible prime and lowered X in the cancelled expression.

- 151_scan071: the written i<j in the prose and i≤j beside (5) are retained separately; the X'-X stroke is unheaded. The lower propre insertion precedes the single existing comma.

- 151_scan072: the marginal correction reads topos élémentaires, replacing C’s first quoted limites proposal; the literal closing reference is (11 d). Both k/h indices in (6), the reworked lower labels and the isolated b/6-like mark remain explicit doubts.

- **151_scan073, (12):** the second V index contains a visible comma, i,j, while the first remains ij; both witnesses omitted that second comma. A separate strike through élémentaires remains within the lower block cancellation. The J′/I′ index and cancelled superscript remain doubtful.

- 151_scan074: the right-end relation has I'_0 subset I', and the repaired subscript in (16) ends U''. The final two V expressions both retain i-prime. The lower group in (16) retains both the cancelled-glyph/lower-replacement and overwritten-U/X/nested-index arrangements; these source forms are transcribed without mathematical replacement.

- **151_scan075, (17):** both left indices have a comma after i-prime and no comma before X-prime; both vertical relations are cups. The singular donné and four-point ending are retained from the visible strokes, while the overlined glyph and two word readings remain doubtful. The separate complete U formula retains equality/superset uncertainty and the overwritten final X/A alternative (audit A75-07).

- **152_scan002:** the fourteen repeated upper labels read w; the three-arc wheel has four black spokes, the small top-right circle has seven long rays, and the lower-left fan has six long directions. The upper label in the framed diameter circle remains tau/Gamma; the lower label is sigma. The crowded lower caption retains w/omega membership W, then u membership W, with both membership signs recorded. G11 retains seven separate dark arrows after the audit removes the unsupported connector.

- **152_scan003:** both upper-right correspondence rows retain the visible non qualification and the uncertain following word. The cut-edge word reads arête. The lower type name reads totalitaire, the K expression retains the crossed empty-set sign, and the lower case condition retains the slash through the subset sign. The cancelled definitions and overwritten 2 remain explicitly doubtful. The compact verb retains équivaut/équiv.-t, and the restored second left definition line retains et/de before S not-equal empty-set.

- **152_scan004:** the margin reads ceux d'ordre 1 aussi; the second ordre is present in the ink. The two Greek forms remain epsilon/xi, the cancelled words remain doubtful, and the live muni is resolved. The large left drawing has eight boundary-to-boundary curves, including the audited pale upper-right U, and its lower inner circle has three contour traces. Four outlined point marks, separate pale arrows/returns and the other audited drawing strokes are retained. The sigma after inv. is recorded as raised and inserted; the isolated point before admissible remains doubtful.

- **152_scan005:** the inserted classes de precedes singular plongement; the lower inserted classe de modifies a second live plongement. The whole U-similarity-R formula remains unresolved. The second symbol of the epsilon pair retains c/varphi/f, and the cancelled glyph before the map retains c/epsilon. The source ends with the literal order bound <=1 and un/une et un seul.

- **152_scan008:** the upper map uses rive and application; the first marginal note has A(C) with a lower plus and orientation. The framed equations retain an inner brace and right border arrow, and the next pair has the second right rg. The later marginal formulas retain their extra relation/vector-A tails with explicit deletion-state doubts; the rho_C expression keeps the separate hook and overwritten upper mark. The upper right-A subscript retains star/leftarrow/plus, and the entire live factor retains sigma_Gamma/id/sigma_0. La définition in the second NB and the final sigma-with-upper-C label are raised insertions. The live a) word remains direct/évident; the first local cancellation remains C/le. The Donc brace has its definite first-row comma.

- **152_scan010:** the correspondence table preserves the overworked second-right sigma/rho glyph, the uncertain raised signs and the rounded tails of the two lower arrows. The inserted short form before pt fixe stays contracted. The lower margin reads rotation-sommets with its hyphen; the upper marginal segmentation retains cela/C le. Delta belongs only to the third-row raised-sign alternatives, while the lower margin retains r/sigma. The right-edge cancellation keeps par with raised la/le/C. The lower statement retains inserted orbites, cancelled paires/points and three separate slightly bending frame paths with open corners/bottom.

- **152_scan011:** the header’s relation has two curved components and is rendered approx; its upper phrase and parenthesis share one baseline. The sigma_C/a placement remains uncertain, while orientations is live and induites belongs to the baseline. The disk drawing retains eight inner curves and four visible outward hatch prongs. The heading reads polygônes with its circumflex. Four local arêtes/côtés groups remain unresolved, along with the faint un/i.e. opening and la/C article; the first l'arête a remains and the second word is l'autre.

- **152_scan013:** the written term is isotypique, with a descending y. The qualifier after arbres plans is interlinear and its leader does not cancel those words. The raised C-like form and its contact with the previous descender remain unresolved. The opening retains donc/dont/dans and the article la/le; the later whole span retains donc/de non/ou non; the crossed phrase retains le contour/qui correspond.

- 151_scan062, independent audit: a small curved mark beside Delta in the cancelled diagram is represented as an unsure upper 2; its exact attachment remains open.
